- **nevermined-router**: Use when an AI agent needs to PAY an external service it does not have an account with — any x402 agent or MPP merchant — using the Nevermined Router. Covers discovering services in the Agent Services Catalog, creating a spending Delegation from an API key, funding the buyer wallet, making paid calls through /api/v1/router/route (or the streaming /proxy), reading the payment ledger, and the guardrails an autonomous buyer must respect. Complements the nevermined-payments skill, which is about RECEIVING payments and buying Nevermined plans. ([nevermined-io/docs](https://github.com/nevermined-io/docs/tree/HEAD/skills/nevermined-router) · ★8)
